# Riot Commander - Roadmap History (archived shipped/closed entries)

## 2026-07-20 - RM-100 / RM-106 / RM-106a / RM-106b full narratives (relocated from ROADMAP)

- **RM-100 ops: the nightly critic + the nightly suite were both silently dead; repaired 2026-07-19 (gemini-loop cycle 1, R130/R131; `aa90e9b4` / `fb88c7d8` / `cf71753f`).** Two independent silent-failure classes, both invisible to the surfaces we actually watch. (a) **RC-GeminiAudit produced nothing for 28 nights while reading Enabled/Ready.** `ops/runtime/gemini_last_audit.txt` pinned a worktree-slice sha that never survived cherry-pick, so `git log <dangling>..HEAD` came back empty and `tools/gemini_audit.ps1` took its own "nothing to audit" branch and exited 0 without writing a review OR a log line - `logs/gemini_audit.log` did not exist at all. Now self-heals to `HEAD~10` with a loud WARN and logs `START` unconditionally as its first write. The separate `0xC000013A` last-run result is **UNPROVEN and still open** - six hypotheses refuted with evidence (TTY/stdin, InteractiveToken teardown, the marker itself, oversized stdin, missing `-NoProfile`, logoff kill), so the task was deliberately NOT re-registered; the new START line makes the next occurrence attributable (START then silence = killed mid-run; no START = died before line 20). **If 0xC000013A recurs on a later nightly, the marker fix is not the cause - read the log.** (b) **The nightly full-suite had been red for 12 consecutive days** (runs 28935372989 .. 29682372934) and nobody saw it, because the push `check` job is green BY CONSTRUCTION - it never runs `tests/` as one process, so it cannot reproduce the pollution. Fixed test-side only: Playwright's session-scoped ProactorEventLoop leaves asyncio's per-thread running-loop marker set on the main thread, breaking any later bare `asyncio.run()`; and the Jhin build-order test depended on a live `:8893` that CI can never spawn (`creationflags=0x08000000` raises on Linux). **Latent, unfixed:** `tests/snapshot_panels` still leaks that marker, so any NEW test calling bare `asyncio.run()` on the main thread and sorting after it will fail identically - use the `_run_coro` / `_run_poll_loop` pattern (now 4 near-identical local copies; consolidating them is the open follow-up). _Id note: this took RM-100, not RM-99 - `docs/DS_SWEEP_TRACKER.md` has RM-99 explicitly reserved as the next free DS GAP id, and an explicit reservation outranks closing the numbering hole._
- **RM-106 SHIPPED 2026-07-19 - the sanctioned pull is BUILT, LIVE-PROVEN, and on the 15-minute schedule.** `tools/rofl_archiver.py --pull` now runs the Match-V5 `/replays` route (`core/rofl_archive.download_replays` + `core/riot_api.get_replay_urls`), resolving each account's PUUID fresh by Riot ID. Measured on the live run: **5 replays downloaded and archived for SamplePlayer#Trist**, and a second run skipped all 5 (idempotent). **FOUR things were only learnable by running it, all now encoded in tests:** (1) the bodies arrive **GZIP-framed**, and urllib does not decompress - the first live pull discarded 5 of 5 as "not a replay" until `_maybe_gunzip` landed; (2) **SamplePlayer#Vayne's five URLs all 404** - Riot LISTS the match but no longer retains the file, which is permanent and expected, so it is counted `gone` and does NOT fail the scheduled run (otherwise `LastTaskResult=1` forever); (3) the two sources spell one match differently (`NA1-x.rofl` from the client, `NA1_x.rofl` from the API), so a filename-keyed skip re-downloads 10 MB - the check is now match-id-keyed across both forms; (4) an S3 body that is not a replay is discarded rather than written, which is what caught (1) instead of poisoning the archive. **The cache defect below is FIXED:** `account:v1:` cache keys now carry a truncated-sha256 fingerprint of the active API key, so a key rotation naturally misses and re-resolves (`test_key_rotation_invalidates_the_account_cache`). **OPERATOR HYPOTHESIS - STILL OPEN, now instrumented rather than guessed:** every pull appends what it saw to `<archive>/pull_log.jsonl`, and `--pull` prints a rotation verdict. Four observations so far, all with NO games played in between, so `rotated=False` proves nothing yet; `pull_rotation_report` deliberately returns `rotated=None` on a single observation rather than fabricating a negative. **Play games, then run `--pull` and read the rotation line** - if the window rotates, the 15-minute cadence already converts it into a permanent archive and no bulk trick is needed. Original measurement record follows. **SUPERSEDED - Riot serves `.rofl` files directly, sanctioned, with an ordinary API key. MEASURED 2026-07-19.** `GET https://americas.api.riotgames.com/lol/match/v5/matches/by-puuid/{puuid}/replays` -> **HTTP 200** `{"total": 5, "matchFileURLs": [...]}`, each a PRE-SIGNED S3 URL on `lol-prod-us-west-2-match-history-replay` with `response-content-disposition=attachment; filename="NA1_xxxx.rofl"`. No third party, no bearer-token scraping, no `.bat`. **Bounds: `X-Amz-Expires=3600` (1 hour) and exactly 5 per account** - a refreshing recency window, not an archive, which is why it PAIRS with `tools/rofl_archiver.py` (pull 5 -> archive permanently -> extract) rather than replacing it. **THE TRAP THAT COST AN HOUR - PUUIDs ARE ENCRYPTED PER API KEY.** Cycling the key invalidates every stored PUUID; all three of ours returned `400 "Bad Request - Exception decrypting <puuid>"`. Worse, `core/riot_api.get_account_by_riot_id` checks `get_cache().get_immutable()` BEFORE any network call (`riot_api.py:346`), so a "re-resolve" silently returns the OLD key's cached PUUID and the 400 persists. Bypassing the cache produced NEW puuid strings and immediate 200s. **CONSEQUENCE, unfixed: every PUUID in `rewind_history.db` (2961 matches) is stale against the API** - still valid as an internal join key, useless as a request parameter - and NOTHING invalidates the immutable cache on key rotation. Fix both before any bulk pull. **ENTITLEMENT, confirmed on the developer portal (app 834837 "Riot Commander"):** `/replays` is an EXPLICITLY APPROVED endpoint of the PRODUCT app, not an undocumented backdoor - which is why the same request 400s with the personal/dev key and 200s with the product key. `API-Key-Riot.txt` must hold the PRODUCT key. Rate limit **20000/10s** (vs 2000/10s on the core Match-V5 routes), so the pull is NOT throughput-constrained - the binding limits are the 5-per-account cap and the 1-hour URL expiry. Do not build backoff for a limit that will never bind. SGP is DEAD as a route (Cloudflare `error code 1010`, and getting past it is bot-detection evasion - not done); its narrative is relocated to `docs/ROADMAP_HISTORY.md`.
  - **RM-106a CLOSED 2026-07-19 - event-mode timelines ARE obtainable, over the LOCAL LCU, with a permanently reduced event set.** `GET /lol-match-history/v1/game-timelines/{gameId}` (lockfile auth, no SGP) measured HTTP 200 / 66.5 KB / 21 frames for a KIWI queue-2400 match. Unlocks the per-minute gold / xp / level curves (s220 PGR gold-diff) + the kill map; does NOT restore item or skill order. **Both former "STILL UNMEASURED" questions are now answered:** the reduced event set is an LCU-TIMELINE property, NOT a KIWI one (a PRACTICETOOL match measured identical), and the history window is HARD-CAPPED AT 20 entries, so this route can NEVER backfill. Full record + verbatim block: `docs/ROADMAP_HISTORY.md` (2026-07-19 session-2 block).
  - **RM-106b** 🟢 **OPEN AND VALIDATED - the ONE EXPERIMENT ran with the flag ON and the answer is YES. This is the ONLY route that recovers ITEM progression for KIWI.** Measured 2026-07-19 against a live KIWI replay with `EnableReplayApi=1` present at `game.cfg:18` (set by LeagueDirector, `C:\Users\Administrator\Documents\LeagueDirector\`): `GET :2999/liveclientdata/allgamedata` -> **HTTP 200 / 19846 bytes**, `gameMode KIWI`, **10 players each carrying `items` / `level` / `runes` / `scores`**. `/replay/{playback,game,particles,render,sequence,recording,banners}` all serve. **SEEK-AND-SAMPLE IS PROVEN, not inferred:** `POST /replay/playback {"time":600.0}` -> `gameTime` measured exactly `600.0`, with Leona level 3 -> 12 and items 4 -> 5 versus the t=26 sample. So per-timestamp item state IS reconstructable by diffing `allPlayers[].items` across seeks - exactly what RM-106a and SGP both CANNOT give. **RETRACTION - do not re-inherit the earlier verdict:** an earlier probe this same session measured HTTP 404 on every `/liveclientdata/*` route and briefly closed RM-106b as NO. That probe ran while `EnableReplayApi` was ABSENT; it measured the flag-OFF state and was WRONG. **TWO REAL LIMITS (measured, not assumed):** `/liveclientdata/activeplayerabilities` returns **HTTP 400** in a replay (no active player in a spectator context), so **SKILL ORDER is NOT recoverable**; and `eventdata` at t=600 carried only `GameStart` / `ChampionKill` / `Multikill` with NO `ITEM_PURCHASED`, so items come from SAMPLING, never from the event stream. **HARD BOUND AMENDED 2026-07-19 (same session) - the "forward-capture only, cannot backfill" line is CONDITIONALLY FALSE and must NOT be re-inherited as written.** It rested on two premises that both failed within the hour: that no old `.rofl` could be obtained, and that no matching old client existed. Measured: a third-party service HOSTS `.rofl` files on its own servers (`GET /lol/download-rofl/{match_id}` plus a short-lived `Authorization: Bearer` JWT carrying `match_id` + `owner`), which delivered a real 10353126-byte `NA1_5595187452.rofl` on patch 16.13 that `core.rofl_archive.extract_stats` parsed cleanly at 18 players x 367 fields; and patch-specific game files are separately obtainable, so its launcher pairs the downloaded `.rofl` with a matching OLD client. **CONSEQUENCE: seek-and-sample CAN backfill history** - any archived match, for any patch a client is kept for, yields per-timestamp item / gold / level state over the same `:2999` route proven above. The limit is that archive's DEPTH plus how many old clients are installed locally, NOT Riot retention. **Still true, unchanged:** Riot's OWN endpoint serves nothing historical (a 16.13 match measured `incompatible` against a 16.14 client), the capture is interactive and NEVER a background job, and `EnableReplayApi=1` must also be set in the OLD client's `game.cfg`. **NOT MEASURED - do not assume:** how far back that archive reaches; test the older ids before planning any bulk backfill. The same service also streams a real server-side client (build stamp `16.13.789.3741 <Releases/16.13>`) under a 45-minute usage quota - a separate route from local playback, which has no such quota. Full record: `docs/ROADMAP_HISTORY.md` (2026-07-19 session-2 block + its correction).

> **CITATION CAVEAT (measured 2026-07-18) - about half the commit hashes cited for
> 2026-04 through 2026-06 do NOT resolve in this repo. This is a known, bounded,
> already-self-corrected defect. Do not chase them.**
>
> Measured across `docs/history_notes.md` + `docs/LEDGER.md` + `docs/ROADMAP_HISTORY.md`:
> 387 of 1148 distinct 8-hex citations (34 percent) fail `git rev-parse --verify`.
> They are NOT scattered rot and NOT a history rewrite - a rewrite would be 100 percent
> before a cutoff and 0 percent after. The real distribution is a steady rate that
> stops dead:
>
> | month | resolvable | missing | miss rate |
> |---|---|---|---|
> | 2026-04 | 1 | 3 | 75% |
> | 2026-05 | 53 | 48 | 47% |
> | 2026-06 | 452 | 457 | 50% |
> | 2026-07 | 537 | 0 | **0%** |
>
> **Cause:** the orchestrator workflow (worktree build agents, one merger) had agents
> commit inside their own worktree, and the merger cherry-picked the result. Entries
> then cited BOTH hashes - e.g. "merge `91b6b847` (slice `03927664`, worktree agent)".
> The merge hash entered `main` and resolves; the slice hash never did and never will.
> `git rev-list --all` equals `HEAD` (3645), so the slice objects are not hiding on
> another ref or in the reflog - they are gone.
>
> **The work still landed.** A dead slice hash does not mean the change is missing; it
> means that entry cited the pre-cherry-pick hash. Verify by the MERGE hash, the file,
> or the test - never by the slice hash.
>
> **Already fixed going forward:** 0 of 537 citations dated 2026-07 are unresolvable, so
> the convention corrected itself. No process change is needed.
>
> **Deliberately NOT repaired in place.** The correct hashes are unrecoverable, and
> rewriting 387 historical citations would violate the append-only rule these files run
> on. This note is additive. It exists because the defect has already cost real work:
> a 2026-07-18 audit spent a full agent trying to resolve `541cd9d3` to disambiguate a
> live-gated row, and that hash is simply gone.

- **RM-37 DS sweep GAP: Lucian crit-sustained mis-served as on-hit + Essence Reaver coherence-docked (kit-modeling fix, NOT a fight_length knob; spec'd, future Tier-2 build).** The sweep's THIRD GAP and a NEW SHAPE (memory `project_ds_sweep_lucian_crit_coherence`; read-only 2026-07-17, engine 1.216.0 / patch 16.14.1, NO engine change made). Lucian DEFAULT-routes carry/ds.dps and that IS his correct home - real meta = crit-auto SUSTAINED DPS: Essence Reaver first (~43% first-completed, ~89% presence, his #1) -> Navori Flickerblade -> Infinity Edge -> Lord Dominik's, Press the Attack keystone; ~70-80% autos via the Lightslinger ability-empowered double-tap + R barrage; NOT lethality-burst, NOT on-hit, NOT ability-caster; ~50.5-51.5% WR A-tier. So unlike Ezreal there is NO archetype mis-route and unlike MF NO burst treatment. But production `rank_for_primary_archetype('Lucian','carry')` leads OFF-META on-hit at BOTH tanky and squishy targets (BotRK #1 / Runaan #2 / Kraken #3 / Stormrazor #4; IE #5-6) and `coherence_rerank` DOCKS his #1 item Essence Reaver from raw #4 to #10-18 (despite ER holding the 2nd-highest raw delta) as a generic cross-archetype artifact. ROOT CAUSE: his Lightslinger double-tap + per-cast Sheen proc is NOT modeled in the DPS/spellblade path (grep `lucian` misses `_passive_damage_overrides.py` / `dps.py` / `abilities.py` / spellblade files), so the sustained-auto integral over-values on-hit AS-stacking and ER's kit-coherence is unrecognized. `rank_for('Lucian', fight_length=0.5)` lifts crit but injects AP pollution (Lich Bane / Rabadon dps=0) AND is the wrong treatment (he is SUSTAINED) - he stays OUT of `_CHAMPION_FIGHT_LENGTH`. FIX (Tier-2, operator-gated): (1) MODEL the Lightslinger empowered double-tap (crit-scaling, on-hit-applying, Sheen-proccing) via the existing aa-empower + spellblade machinery (likely `_passive_damage_overrides.py` / `abilities.py`); (2) stop coherence_rerank docking ER for Lucian (kit_synergy recognizes the Sheen + double-tap synergy, or a per-champ exemption); (3) RED-first tests (Lucian top-8 surfaces ER/IE/Navori/crit, no BotRK/Runaan lead, zero AP; Varus/Ashe/Aphelios + the fight_length cohort + generic crit-ADC ER-dock byte-identical); (4) ENGINE bump + Share + DS restart + live eyeball. A sim-incoherent-build modeling gap (governed by `project_ds_build_reco_optimal_not_winrate`), NOT win-rate chasing.

Production `rank_for_primary_archetype('Ahri','mage')` leads OFF-META Liandry's Torment 6653 (sustained %HP DoT) #1 at EVERY target incl squishy (meta builds Liandry's ONLY vs tanks) + Blackfire Torch 2503 #2, and BURIES her signature Malignance 3118 at #13-15. Production `rank_for_primary_archetype('Ambessa','bruiser')` leads OFF-META **Blade of the Ruined King 3153 (on-hit) #1** by ~2x at every target/level + Trinity #2 / Kraken / Essence Reaver / IE, and buries her signature Spear of Shojin 3161 at **rank 47/80** (Black Cleaver #25, Death's Dance #34, Sundered Sky #14); a whitelist probe of ONLY her 6 core items still ranks BotRK #1 (124) and Spear of Shojin DEAD LAST #6 (19.78). Root cause: the hybrid DPS sub-model integrates SUSTAINED AUTO-ATTACK dps (so on-hit/AS win), and her real damage engine (bonus-AD Q/W/E/R + ability haste for cast frequency) is unmodeled so Shojin/ability-bruiser items score ~zero on the DPS term. NOT a pool gap (Shojin in-pool at #47 / #6-whitelist - DISTINCT from Ezreal/Corki), NOT burst/fight_length (sustained skirmish - DISTINCT from MF), NOT a coherence dock (the mis-order is in the hybrid DPS sub-model - DISTINCT from Lucian). FIX (Tier-2, operator-gated, BATCH with RM-39): (1) MODEL her bonus-AD abilities as primary AD-axis damage + credit ABILITY HASTE (cast frequency) in the ds.hybrid DPS term (reuse/extend the Aatrox RM-39 melee-ability-haste-axis fix) so Shojin/Black Cleaver/Death's Dance/Eclipse rise + on-hit/crit fall; (2) NOT a pool add, NOT fight_length, NOT a coherence change; (3) RED-first tests (Ambessa top-8 = Eclipse/Shojin/Death's Dance/Sundered Sky, no BotRK/Kraken/on-hit lead, zero crit/AS; Aatrox shares the fix - verify jointly; carry cohort byte-identical); (4) GENERALIZE - one fix covers the AD-ability-bruiser cohort (Aatrox/Ambessa + spot-probe Camille/Renekton/Riven/Sett/Irelia/Jax/Darius); (5) ENGINE bump + Share mirror + DS :8893 restart + live eyeball.

- **RM-44 DS sweep GAP (NEW shape): Amumu magic-damage AP-scaling tank - the dominant Liandry's-first hybrid AP-rush is unmodeled by the axis-neutral ds.ehp scorer (spec'd, future Tier-2 build).** The sweep's TENTH GAP and the FIRST in the TANK (ds.ehp) scorer (memory `project_ds_sweep_amumu_magic_tank_ap_rush`; tracker `docs/DS_SWEEP_TRACKER.md`; read-only 2026-07-17, engine 1.216.0 / patch 16.14.1, NO engine change made). Amumu DEFAULT-routes tank/ds.ehp (tags [Tank,Support], kit axis AP magic 0.773, `default_for_champion`=('tank','enchanter'); tank is NEVER axis-corrected - `_ARCHETYPE_AXIS['tank']=None`, axis-neutral by design). Production `rank_for_primary_archetype('Amumu','tank')` leads PURE DURABILITY (Randuin's 3143 #1, Warmog's #2, Heartsteel #3, Kaenic Rookern #4, Jak'Sho #5) and NEVER surfaces Liandry's 6653 (not an EHP item), ranks his highest-WR Abyssal Mask 8020 only #14; the mage-route counterfactual leads full-AP (Liandry's/Blackfire/Rabadon's) which is OFF-META. A player following the engine builds pure tank and misses his signature first-item AP rush. NOT pure durability (Alistar builds ZERO damage -> REFUTE; Amumu explicitly rushes one AP item), NOT a full-AP flip (off-meta). Governed by `project_ds_build_reco_optimal_not_winrate`: the pure-EHP reco is NOT his optimal build (the Liandry's-first hybrid is ~all games + highest WR). - **RM-45 DS sweep GAP (batch with RM-40): Annie ult-haste burst mage - sustained-DoT over-led + Malignance under-valued on ds.ability (spec'd, future Tier-2 build).** The sweep's ELEVENTH GAP and the SECOND in the MAGE (ds.ability) scorer - the same shape as Ahri RM-40 (memory `project_ds_sweep_annie_burst_malignance`; tracker `docs/DS_SWEEP_TRACKER.md`; read-only 2026-07-17, engine 1.216.0 / patch 16.14.1, NO engine change made). Annie DEFAULT-routes mage/ds.ability (tags [Mage,Support], kit axis AP magic 0.902, NOT in `_AP_ASSASSIN_IDS`) - the correct axis + archetype. Her real meta (HIGH conf, 4 aggregators + kit) is a classic AP BURST combo mage (stack Pyromania -> guaranteed stun -> Q+W+R Tibbers all-in one-combo delete): **Malignance LEADS first item** (clear by presence AND WR) -> Sorcerer's Shoes -> Rocketbelt/Stormsurge -> Shadowflame -> Zhonya's, Electrocute ~72%, AP > ability haste > magic-pen; Tibbers' persistent aura is NEGLIGIBLE (~16 + 4% AP/sec) = burst payload not sustained-DoT; Liandry's/Blackfire/Rylai's OFF-META for her. Production `rank_for_primary_archetype('Annie','mage')` leads OFF-META Liandry's Torment 6653 (sustained %HP-DoT) #1 by ~1.6x + Blackfire Torch 2503 #2, and BURIES her signature first item Malignance 3118 at #15-16 (Luden's #12, Rabadon's #3 + Shadowflame #4 DO surface correctly). Root cause: ds.ability optimizes SUSTAINED single-target magic DPS, over-reads Tibbers' negligible aura + W's AoE as continuous damage so it over-leads Liandry's/Blackfire, and never credits ult-haste -> more Tibbers casts -> more burst (Malignance's raison d'etre). FIX (Tier-2, operator-gated, BATCH with RM-40): (1) model ult-haste/ability-haste as cast-frequency in ds.ability so Malignance rises for combo-burst mages + credit its per-ult proc; (2) dampen sustained-%HP-DoT over-valuation for BURST-combo mages (a burst/short-window AP weighting, the AP-side analogue of the MF fight_length treatment, shared with the Ahri RM-40 fix); (3) do NOT reroute to ds.burst blind (AD-polluted per RM-41) - fix the mage scorer; (4) RED-first tests (Annie top-6 = Malignance/Luden's/Shadowflame/Rabadon's, Liandry's NOT #1, zero AD; sustained-DoT mages Anivia/Brand/Malzahar KEEP Liandry's high - Anivia is the explicit control; controls byte-identical); (5) GENERALIZE - the Ahri/Annie burst-mage cohort (Lux/Syndra/Veigar/Zoe/Xerath/Vex/Hwei) vs the Anivia/Brand/Malzahar sustained-DoT cohort; (6) ENGINE bump + Share mirror + DS :8893 restart + live eyeball.

Production `rank_for_primary_archetype('Ashe','carry')` leads NOT-BUILT on-hit HARD - Blade of the Ruined King 3153 #1 (by ~2x), Runaan's Hurricane 3085 #2, Kraken Slayer #3 - over her crit-AS core, surfaces IE only #6, buries Hexoptics C44 2523 at #14 and Phantom Dancer 3046 at #21 (DEAD-LAST #6 even in a whitelist of her own 6 core items). DISTINCT root cause vs Akshan's double-shot: (1) her Frost passive means crits deal NO bonus damage - crit-CHANCE converts to flat bonus AD, so IE's crit-strike multiplier is largely DEAD on her while crit-chance-for-stat is her real crit value; (2) Ranger's Focus Q is a multi-arrow AS-steroid flurry the ds.dps auto-integral mis-reads as generic on-hit uptime, inflating BotRK/Runaan's/Kraken. Routing her to ds.onhit ALSO over-leads on-hit (probed) - carry IS her home. The CONTRAST twin of Aphelios REFUTE (both crit-marksmen; Aphelios crits deal FULL damage so ds.dps ranks his crit core right, Ashe crits dead so it escapes to on-hit). FIX (Tier-2, operator-gated; batch the crit-marksman family RM-37/42 + add Ashe-specific modeling): (1) MODEL Frost (crits deal no bonus damage, crit-chance -> flat AD) so the auto-integral stops crediting IE's crit-strike multiplier + credits crit-chance for the AD conversion; (2) MODEL Ranger's Focus as an attack-speed steroid (not a per-arrow on-hit multiplier) so BotRK/Runaan's stop over-scaling; net: lift Phantom Dancer/Hexoptics/IE above generic not-built on-hit; (3) RED-first tests (Ashe top-6 = Hexoptics/PD/IE/LDR, no BotRK/Runaan's lead, Phantom Dancer NOT dead-last in her own core; Aphelios full-crit + Varus/Kai'Sa on-hit-primary byte-identical; Akshan/Lucian share the crit-marksman batch - verify their double-shot fix is orthogonal to Ashe's Frost fix); (4) ENGINE bump + Share mirror + DS :8893 restart + live eyeball.

- ✅ **item 315 (2026-06-05; DS engine SCHEMA LIFT but NO ENGINE bump - byte-identical live, ENGINE stays 1.118.0; DS NOT restarted; non-frozen) - P3.2: antitank.py dynamic %HP scaling via optional injected ResolvedStats.** Operator-accepted design change (item 312 had flagged antitank as intentionally STATIC per item 308). SEEDED Gwen P (ap_ratio 0.0005) + KogMaw W (ap_ratio 0.0004); the AD path is proven via a synthetic test entry (no champion mis-seeded with a fake AD term). PURELY ADDITIVE: `stats=None` (the default, and what the `/anti-tank` route passes) is byte-identical to item 308, as is any zero-ratio row even WITH stats injected -> NO ENGINE bump, NO live-output change, NO downstream caller change (tight blast radius per the operator). Live math verified: Gwen 0.85 static -> 0.95 @200AP / 0.85 @999AD (AP-only correct); KogMaw 0.926 -> 0.966 @200AP; Vayne 0.95 byte-identical @800AP/800AD. ## Shipped/closed - relocated from Open items - Medium priority

- ✅ **DS review + augments - bonus-AD-zeroing augment: VERIFIED not an RC bug (2026-05-17 - do not re-investigate).** External report (different League project): an Arena augment ("Adapt") zeroes bonus AD → Maw shield / Endless Hunger haste / Death's Dance heal floor out; reporter hedged it might be a modeling-accuracy nit. **Confirmed against `effects.py`:** RC models all three as `defensive_only` / **non-DPS** - Maw `3156` (lifeline magic shield, no DPS), Death's Dance `6333` (bleed, no DPS proc), Endless Hunger `2517`+`222517` (Famine bonus-AD→haste + Feast omnivamp explicitly tagged *"utility; no DPS contribution"*). They contribute **zero** to DPS ranking regardless of bonus AD, so a bonus-AD-zeroing augment cannot make DS over- or under-rank them - there is no bonus-AD-scaled credit to distort. The one lifeline-family item RC *does* credit with DPS, Sterak's Gage `3053` "Claws that Catch", is correctly keyed off **base AD** (`bonus_ad_pct_base_ad=0.45`), not bonus AD - also immune. EHP path likewise gives no credit: shield throughput is an explicit Phase-1.5 deferral in `ehp.py` (lifeline family handled only as a dead-unique dedup, never bonus-AD-scaled EHP). **The other project's diagnosis does not lift to RC.** The real, *separate* gap is the already-tracked Phase-1.5 shield/heal throughput + P1b Arena-augment `as_pct` overlay (CLAUDE #26c) - RC currently gives no Arena pick-credit for these defensive items at all, a deliberate modeling boundary, not a bonus-AD over-rank bug.

- ✅ **item 209b - gamepc_phase_watcher.py capture reliability - drop WaitingForStats + InProgress edge delay SHIPPED 2026-05-27 (`6f56cd6`, pushed origin/main `8d36960..6f56cd6`; non-frozen; Game-PC patched binary deployed + RC-PhaseWatcher restarted pid 5128 -> 14320).** OPERATOR DIAGNOSIS: the item 207 LCU phase watcher fires DXGI capture (both monitors via bettercam) on the `WaitingForStats` gameflow phase, which lands at the exact moment League swaps 1920x1080 -> 1440p. A bound DXGI grab at the 1920x1080 <-> 1440p resolution swap is unreliable (the same reason the continuous DXGI screen-agent was retired in favor of the in-process self-grab relay (1-PC, ADR-011)). The `Matchmaking -> InProgress` transition edge is the symmetric game-start swap risk. **Surgical fix:** `_CAPTURED_GAMEFLOW_PHASES: drop "WaitingForStats"` (keep ChampSelect + InProgress); NEW `INPROGRESS_CAPTURE_DELAY_S = 15.0` module constant; `handle_event` sleeps past the game-start resolution swap before binding DXGI when classified topic is gameflow_phase/InProgress. Cherry augment_select stays (mid-game stable resolution). 65/65 tests PASS (+2/-1). **Carries forward:** future event-driven captures on Game-PC MUST exclude any phase that coincides with a resolution swap. The discrete-event mental model ("only fires once at game end") is wrong - the trigger is the resolution swap co-firing with a bound DXGI surface (an unreliable grab).

- ✅ **item 209a - bridge_watcher CREATE_NO_WINDOW on watcher claude.cmd spawns SHIPPED 2026-05-27 (`8d36960`, pushed origin/main `07f5e79..8d36960`; non-frozen + 1 operator-granted frozen file).** Operator-reported: cmd window keeps opening on Legion. Mirrors the flag pattern at `tools/legion_bridge_daemon.py:212`. NEW `tests/test_bridge_watcher_create_no_window.py` 3 drift guards. - ✅ **KEYSTONE - champ-select wrong for ARAM-Mayhem: FIXED + PROVEN LIVE** (2026-05-17, commit `3eb2e2d`; surrender-slice `a5405ee`). **Root cause was NOT phase-timing/retention/router** (the hand-off's leading hypothesis) - it was a flat **queue-ID gap**: ARAM Mayhem reports `queueId=2400` (KIWI, documented s220 from real match data), but `core/queue_modes.QUEUE_ID_TO_MODE_KEY` only mapped `920` (Poro King - the codebase had conflated 920↔2400) and the agent's `is_aram = queue_id in (450,920)` likewise omitted 2400 → `is_aram=False` → JS `_csvDetectMode` fell to "sr", bench/quick-swap never rendered; `_preflip_mode_from_lcu(2400)→None` → `mode_key` stuck at "client". - ✅ **s228 - DS Phase 5.9.28: conditional-target-state block_index schema lift - Part 1** (2026-05-16, ENGINE 0.99.0→**1.0.0**). The s225/s226/s227 hand-offs flagged pure-data registry work as **exhausted** (5 iterations, all 4 override registries swept) and the conditional-target-state schema lift as the only remaining DS vein - architectural, reserved for sign-off. Surfaced via `AskUserQuestion`; **operator chose option B** (the multi-session lift). This is **Part 1**, scoped exactly like s207's `int→int|list[int]` lift (schema + flagship seeds first; bulk seeding + live plumbing are follow-ups). **Schema:** `block_index` value widens `int | list[int]` → ALSO `dict[str, int|list[int]]` mapping a target-state condition → block; `"default"` (required) = the operator-commits/canonical-amped branch (s191 model); other keys = positive live-target-state descriptors selecting a **downgrade**. **Closed vocab** `_BLOCK_INDEX_CONDITIONS = {target_full_hp, target_no_cc}` (mirrors `_BLOCK_STRATEGIES`); unknown key **raises**. **Part-1 resolver:** `_select_blocks` resolves any conditional dict to `"default"` **unconditionally** - byte-identical to the equivalent int/list (**provably zero regression**); live predicate evaluation is **Part 2**. Server `_parse_block_index` accepts well-formed conditional bodies + skips malformed defensively (untrusted-body no-500; engine validator = hard enforcer for the trusted registry). **3 flagship seeds - all CONVERSIONS of shipped unconditional entries** (Part-1 provably no-op): **Zoe E** `{default:2,target_no_cc:0}` (live A/B 140.00 reg==forced{E:2}, 2× block-0 70.00), **Evelynn Q** `{default:5,target_no_cc:0}` (345.00==forced{Q:5}, 7.7× block-0; R:1 preserved), **Kindred E** `{default:1,target_full_hp:0}` (80.00 reg==forced{E:1}==forced{E:0} - honest full-HP no-op; the 7.5/5% missing-HP delta is Part-2's signal). Caller conditional dict via wire proven. Registry stays **125 champions** (in-place conversions). New `test_conditional_block_index_s228.py` (~52 tests) + `test_block_index_overrides` shape-pins migrated (incl. `test_every_value_*`→`_int_list_or_conditional`, mirrors s207) + 5 s223-s226 backward-compat pins updated to assert new shape + Part-1 equivalence (s223 KindredE class's 4 semantic tests pass UNCHANGED) + 9 ENGINE pin bumps. DS suite 2136→**2188**; wider RC **1107**; ruff+py_compile clean; DS restarted (taskkill 13452 → relaunch) → `/health` **1.0.0**. **NEXT (option B continues):** **Part 2** - thread real liveclient target HP%/CC into the ranking call (`coach_integration/archetype_dispatch.py` + state-builder + `/api/ds-preview`; `target_full_hp` has a clean liveclient signal, `target_no_cc` stays commits-default until a CC source exists). Plus a fast pure-data conditional seed-expansion batch (s207→s215/s217 pattern): Lux Illumination / Aatrox W chain-landed / Fiddle Q fear-state / more sleep/charm/mark amps. **DrMundo E is caster-missing-HP, not target** - needs a `caster_low_hp` vocab extension, flag for operator. s220 aggregator G Post-Game-Review reframe remains the big pending UI item.

First of six archetype scorers per `NEXT_SESSION_PLAN_2026-05-12_ARCHETYPE_EXPANSION.md` (Phase 2 = Bruiser hybrid, Phase 3 = CS picker UI, Phase 4 = Mage ability DPS, Phase 5 = Assassin burst, Phase 6 = Enchanter HPS). Phase 1 deferrals to 1.5: shield throughput, healing throughput, caster-side enemy pen.

- ✅ **s175 - Phase 2 Bruiser hybrid scorer** (2026-05-12). Second of the six archetype scorers. New `agents/daemon_slayer/hybrid.py` composes `compute_dps()` + `compute_ehp()` into `hybrid_score = α·dps + β·ehp`: `compute_hybrid()` + `HybridResult` for the scorer, `rank_items_by_hybrid()` + `HybridRankedItem` + `HybridRankResult` for the ranker. Per-champion (α,β) overrides ship in new `agents/daemon_slayer/archetype_weights.json` - 20 bruiser entries (Jarvan IV 0.55/0.45, Darius 0.65/0.35, Riven 0.70/0.30, Mordekaiser 0.50/0.50, ... full list in CLAUDE.md item 32) + default fallback (0.50, 0.50). Ranker sort key is normalized percentage delta (`α · dps_delta/baseline_dps + β · ehp_delta/baseline_ehp`) so weights stay intuitive across the ~10× DPS/EHP magnitude gap. New `/hybrid` + `/rank-bruiser` server routes + optional `alpha`/`beta` override params + `rank_bruiser_for()` / `hybrid_for()` client helpers + `BruiserRankedItem` dataclass. ENGINE_VERSION 0.63.0 → 0.64.0. Live-verified on Legion :8893: Jarvan IV α=0.55 picks Trinity Force first; Nasus with override α=0.2/β=0.8 surfaces Heartsteel + Warmog's in top 5. Tests: 44 new (40 hybrid + 4 server HybridRouteTests) → 1066 DS suite (was 1022); wider RC 839 green. **Next: Phase 3 = CS scorer-picker UI** (single session, pure UI, exposes 6-archetype dropdown in champ-select + in-game switch).

New `core/archetype_picks.py` owns the data layer (DDragon-tag → archetype mapping, per-champion override persistence in `data/cs_archetype_picks.json`, `get_archetype_for()` merger). New `dashboard/routes_archetype.py` exposes `GET/POST /api/cs-archetype-pick`. New `rank_for_primary_archetype()` dispatcher in `core/daemon_slayer_client.py` routes carry → ds.dps, bruiser → ds.hybrid, tank → ds.ehp; mage/assassin/enchanter fall through to ds.dps with `fell_back=True` until Phases 4-6 ship dedicated scorers. New 6-button 3×2 picker grid in `web/js/panels/champ_select.js` My Pick card; clicks save to `localStorage.rc-cs-archetype-<champion>` + POST to persist. Live: `curl /api/cs-archetype-pick?champion=Aatrox` returns DDragon default; POST persists; clear rolls back.

- ✅ **s184 - First-purchase archetype-mismatch soft-nudge** (2026-05-13). Closes the s176 Phase 3 deferral. New `core/archetype_mismatch.py` (~270 LOC) owns the evaluator: `compute_nudge_payload(coach, lc, lcu_snapshot, cs_archetype_pick)` is called once per /api/state and returns the `state.archetype_nudge` payload. Mismatch detection is dispatcher-driven (no hardcoded item→archetype affinity table) - calls `rank_for_primary_archetype(champion, primary, top=15)` once per (champion, game_session) pair and stamps "fired" when the operator's first non-trivial completed item isn't in the top-15 ids. Item filter denylist covers boots/Doran's/trinkets/consumables/SR-starters/jungle-pets (~28 IDs); operator's other 99% of items qualify as signal. Per-game-session dedup via liveclient's `gameData.gameId` (with synthetic `champion@start-floor` fallback); module-level threadsafe cache, restart-wipes. Default DDragon-tag picks skip evaluation (`source="default"` → no nudge) - only fires when operator explicitly picked or accepted a prior nudge. Three states: `pending` (no first item yet), `no_mismatch` (item in top-15, decision cached), `fired` (mismatch). Engine-down returns empty payload + leaves cache untouched so the next poll retries when DS comes back. `dashboard/_liveclient.py` extended with `owned_item_ids` (parallel int-id list to `owned_items` names) + `game_id` siblings. `dashboard/_state_builder.py` stamps `state.archetype_nudge` next to `state.cs_archetype_pick`. Two new routes in `dashboard/routes_archetype.py`: `GET /api/archetype-nudge` (diagnostic snapshot) + `POST /api/archetype-nudge/dismiss {champion}` (operator clicked the chip's X - idempotent; flips phase to "dismissed"). `dashboard/api_schema.py` gains `ArchetypeNudgeDismissRequest` + `ArchetypeNudgePayload` models. ENGINE_VERSION 0.69.0 unchanged - no DS engine math touched. Tests: 54 new (40 in `test_archetype_mismatch.py` covering item-filter denylist + session-token + engine-mode mapper + 12 nudge-evaluator cases (happy / dedup / engine-down / default-source-skipped / pending / re-eval on new session) + dismiss + NudgeResult shape + 5 dispatcher-boundary cases; 5 in `test_state_builder_archetype_nudge.py` covering build_state stamp + empty + pending passthrough + exception-resilience; 9 in `test_routes_archetype_nudge.py` covering GET snapshot + POST happy/400/empty paths + route-registration smoke) → 1009 wider RC (was 955); DS suite unchanged 1426. Live-verified end-to-end on Legion :8888 after `echo restart > restart_trigger.txt`: `GET /api/archetype-nudge` returns `{"ok": true, "state": {}}`; `POST /api/archetype-nudge/dismiss` handles happy + 400 + idempotent paths; `/api/state.archetype_nudge` present (empty since no game). Dashboard JS chip renderer **deferred to s184.1** - backend-first ship matches s174-s183 cadence; operator can verify via raw `/api/state` until the chip lands.

- ✅ **s192 - Phase 5.9.5 token-variant block_index for Akali R** (2026-05-14). Closes s191 carry-forward (a). Akali R has 3 Meraki blocks - block 0 base + bonus-AD scaling (R1), block 1 minimum-HP R2, block 2 maximum-HP R2 via missing-HP curve. Pre-s192 the burst walker only knew base keys so setting `{"Akali": {"R": 2}}` globally would over-count R1. Extended the walker to do TOKEN-CANONICAL lookup first (`block_overrides.get("R2")`), falling back to BASE-KEY lookup (`block_overrides.get("R")`). API surface stayed `dict[str, int]`; valid-key set extended from `{Q,W,E,R}` to also include `{Q2,W2,E2,R2}`. Forward-compatible - future per-token entries drop in without code changes. Registry gains `"Akali": {"R": 0, "R2": 2}`. ENGINE_VERSION 0.76.0 → 0.77.0. Tests: 7 new in new `AkaliTokenVariantTests` class + extended valid-key assertion → DS suite 1658 → 1665; wider RC 1013 green. Live A/B on :8893: Akali burst 787.9 (forced R+R2:0) vs **941.7 (registry)** vs 1095.6 (naive R:2 only - over-counts R1) - registry +19.5% delta sits exactly halfway, confirming token-variant model captures the realistic mid-point. /rank-assassin top: **Lich Bane #1 +396.54** (Spellblade × doubled R2 max-execute × 90% AP scaling). `compute_ability_dps` unaffected - R2 entry is invisible to the mage scorer's Q/W/E/R-only iteration. **Carried forward to Phase 5.9.6:** conditional block_index based on target state (Zoe sleep amp, Lux Illumination, DrMundo E missing-HP threshold, Renekton Q Fury - substantial schema lift; defer until 3+ candidates accumulate).

- ✅ **s191 - Phase 5.9 per-(champion, key) damage block_index overrides** (2026-05-14). Closes a long-standing systematic under-scoring: 248 multi-block (champion, key, form) tuples in `champion_abilities.json` carry block ≥1 with amped/empowered/max/total damage; pre-s191 `block_strategy="first"` locked every caller to block 0. New `agents/daemon_slayer/champion_block_index.json` seed registry (12 entries across 11 champions): Cassiopeia E=1 (Total Enhanced vs poisoned), Veigar R=1 (Maximum vs executed), Anivia E=1 (Enhanced vs chilled), Diana W=2 (all 3 Pale Cascade orbs), Brand W=1 + R=1, Evelynn R=1 (Empowered sub-30% HP), Aurora Q=2, Belveth E=2, Karma W=1, Vex R=2, Ahri Q=1. Loader + resolver in `ability_dps.py` mirror the Phase 4e form_index pattern (`_BLOCK_INDEX_PATH` + singleton-cached `_load_block_index_table` + `get_block_index_for` + `_resolve_block_index_overrides` + `reset_block_index_cache`). `_select_blocks` extended with new `"indexed"` strategy + `block_index: int = 0` param (clamps negative→0, out-of-range→last). `compute_ability_dps` / `rank_items_by_ability_dps` / `compute_burst_damage` / `rank_items_by_burst` all gain `block_index_overrides: Optional[dict[str, int]] = None` arg + per-spell loop switches to `"indexed"` strategy per-key when override present (others honor global `block_strategy`). All 4 result types gain `block_index_source: str` + `block_index_resolved: dict[str, int]` surfaced in `to_dict()`. - ✅ **RC-DaemonSlayer task context - RESOLVED 2026-05-17 (`ef30b6f`, code fix, kept SYSTEM+startup).** Root cause: `tools/start_daemon_slayer.py:_log_startup` wrote only to `logs/` and did `except OSError: pass`, so under the SYSTEM-context task a boot-time write failure vanished. Rejected the proposed ops change (SYSTEM→Administrator + startup→LogonTrigger) - DS server is **not** supervisor-watched, so a logon trigger would leave it down on an unattended reboot (availability regression to buy traceability). Instead made logging resilient: canonical `logs/` → ProgramData fallback (`_FALLBACK_LOG_FILE`, always SYSTEM-writable) → best-effort stderr echo (`sys.stderr` may be unusable under pythonw - swallowed); `_log_startup` now returns the `Path` written or `None` and never raises. TDD'd (`tests/phase7_polish/test_start_daemon_slayer_logging.py`, 8 tests + 2 helper tests; full phase7_polish 43 green; ruff clean). Live-smoked: launcher's port-bound guard line landed in the real log **and** stderr. No scheduled-task change; no reboot needed.

RED-first TDD (+9 pure +2 wire), 621 hot-path green, Tier-1 additive, no engine/ENGINE/Share/flip; RC restarted clean pid 17540. **L3 DONE (item 629, 2026-06-26):** Dynamic objective-respawn fix SHIPPED. `core.event_callouts._objective_callouts` now promotes the `last_kill + respawn` math into the served `next_callouts` path: once an elemental drake (last kill + 300s) or Baron (last kill + 360s) is taken, the ETA tracks the REAL take instead of the static game-start grid; Baron also gains a real RESPAWN ETA the old one-shot schedule never had; when Soul is secured (>= 4 elemental drakes) the elemental-drake row is SUPPRESSED (Elder pit). - 🟡 **ZOI minimap overlay program (item 567, 2026-06-21).** The Zone-of-Influence minimap overlay, grounded on a NO-API coordinate source: League `game.cfg` `[HUD]` MinimapScale/FlipMiniMap geometry (NOT the coordinate-less Live Client :2999). **FOUNDATION + SLICES 1+2+3 DONE:** (a) `w-mmrect` settings-driven click-through gold-outline box (`94b31bad`); (b) slice-1 fixes `f93f5f01` - the box now RENDERS over a live game (threaded `minimap_rect` into client `state.latest` at all 3 poll sites; the ui_mock test had masked the gap) and FRAMES the minimap (overlay window sized to full display BOUNDS not the taskbar-excluded work area -> ovscale 1.33; re-assert bounds after topmost -> winH 1440 so the bottom is reachable; outline 1px/0.55 -> 2px/0.85 gold); live-verified box vs minimap teal frame within ~1px; (c) slice-2 `d6a853f2` - `core/minimap_blob_detect.py` pure-numpy team-color blob detection (saturation discriminates icon vs terrain tint; 8-connected flood-fill centroids; `crop_minimap` maps design-px rect onto the ~1280-wide frame by fraction) -> `/api/state.minimap_dots` (mode + in-game gated, TTL-cached, fail-soft); 11 tests, live 32 dots in ~1.6ms. **SLICE 3 DONE** (`163b76ee`): low-opacity team-colored ZOI bubbles + a weighted-bisector demarcation render inside the box (canvas; PER-TEAM offscreen compositing caps each team at alpha 0.25, EMA-smoothed, click-through pointer-events:none), one bubble per dot, radius/weight scaled by RC per-team strength (dot presence + MY ult spikes 6/11/16 + game_time), and a deterministic map-control callout feeds the coach (`det.callouts`, no Haiku). Honest scope: strength is PRESENCE-grounded - the Live Client exposes no enemy level/alive/gold; `blue=ally/red=enemy` = League default colors. Live-verified via CDP (29 bubbles painting over the real minimap, click-through confirmed, 99.7% pixels <= 0.25); UI-audit SHIP-CLEAN. **NEXT (future, no owner; PARKED 2026-06-21 cont7 - LOW value + regression risk, do NOT build blind):** champion-only isolation (template matching) vs the presence detector - `core/minimap_blob_detect.py:16-19` author note says "total team presence is the right input anyway" for a ZOI map-control signal, and a pure-numpy template match on a noisy ~312px crop would REPLACE the live-verified presence detector (slices 1-3); two Gemini passes said do not attempt without operator sign-off. Richer strength if an enemy-data source appears. **LIVE-RENDER FIXED 2026-06-30 (item 688):** the slice-3 "live-verified via CDP" claim was WRONG - the shading never actually painted in the live overlay because the `#am-zoi-canvas` rendered at computed `opacity:0` (a Chromium quirk for an absolute child inside the `position:fixed`+zoomed `#am-mmrect`); forced `canvas.style.opacity="1"` (`8f149677`), raised MAX_ALPHA 0.25->0.55, and blurred the team layers so bubbles merge into soft zones (`9c8fe7f8`). LIVE pending operator alpha/blur tuning; ARAM has no demarcation so its ally-flood tint never paints (blurred bubbles are the ARAM coloring). **NATIVE-RES GRAB SHIPPED + PER-CHAMPION DETECTION = CONFIRMED DEAD-END 2026-07-01 (item 711, `f9ebcd3f`):** `current_minimap_dots` now prefers a native full-screen grab cropped to the minimap (~416px, 4x pixels, no JPEG; `RC_ZOI_NATIVE_GRAB` default ON) - the resolution lever the operator asked for. Only template-matching champion PORTRAITS could isolate them (major risky CV build, operator sign-off gated). ## 2026-07-19 - RM-99 / R137 Heartsteel permanent-HP half (relocated from ROADMAP.md)

_Relocated verbatim when ROADMAP.md crossed its 81920-byte CI budget. SHIPPED at ENGINE 1.226.0; RM-99b (the damage half's wrong 3.5s cadence) was deliberately NOT relocated - it is still OPEN in ROADMAP.md._

- **RM-99 SHIPPED 2026-07-19 (R137, ENGINE 1.226.0) - DEFAULT-OFF `assume_item_health_stacks`.**
  See the R137 sub-bullet at the end of this item for what shipped and for THREE spec errors in
  the research narrative below (the 8% coefficient is WRONG, the fold site is under-specified, and
  the prescribed R46 plumbing is route-unreachable). Original filing, corrections inline:
  **Heartsteel 3084 permanent-HP half is pinned at ZERO stacks, forever.** Meraki
  "Colossal Consumption" grants permanent bonus health equal to 8% of the empowered proc damage
  (30s cooldown per target). Only the damage half is modelled; `_effects_data.py` disclaims the
  rest verbatim in-line - "The HP-on-damage permanent stack (8% of damage as max HP) is not
  modeled - that's stat-side, not proc-side". No registry credits it. The structural reason is
  the familiar one: `_passive_health_overrides.passive_health_stack_hp` (R46) is EXACTLY this
  axis but is keyed by `(champion_id, ability_key, form_index)`, so an item can never match it,
  and the two item-side HP-numerator lanes that do exist (`_item_mana_health` R105,
  `_item_bonus_hp_amp` R107) are both exact conversions of an already-resolved stat and cannot
  express `procs x hp_per_proc`. Measured live on Sion L13 `[3047, 3068]` via `/rank-tank`:
  Heartsteel sits #3 (2016.80 delta EHP) behind Randuin's 2731.28 and Warmog's 2240.88, each
  proc is worth ~43 EHP, so ~5.2 procs passes Warmog's and ~16.5 takes #1 - ordinary mid-game
  numbers for the item whose entire identity is its stack counter. **Not RM-91** (that is
  HP-not-priced-as-damage and needs an objective change; this is a pure max-HP pool add landing
  inside `ds.ehp`'s existing objective) and **not RM-94** (Mejai's is pinned at MAX stacks, this
  at ZERO). Fix shape: `_item_health_stack.py` + DEFAULT-OFF `assume_item_health_stacks`, folded
  next to `item_bonus_hp_amp_hp`, with a monotone assumed-procs-by-level curve copying the
  stack-count-proxy rationale already written in `_passive_health_overrides.py`. S/M, no schema
  lift. Secondary, offense-side: `every_n_seconds=3.5` drops Meraki's explicit 30s-per-target
  cooldown, so a single-target rotation gets ~8.57x the real proc count - the same proxy number
  serves both.
  - **R137 SHIPPED 2026-07-19 (ENGINE 1.226.0): `_item_health_stack.py` + DEFAULT-OFF
    `assume_item_health_stacks`, folded into the three main per-type EHP numerators.** Registered
    3084 AND the Arena mirror 223084 (which the filing above omits entirely). Route-surfaced on
    `/ehp`, `/rank-tank`, `/hybrid`, `/rank-bruiser`. DS suite 8787 passed / 1 skipped / 2461
    subtests. **THREE ERRORS IN THE PARAGRAPH ABOVE - do not inherit them.** (1) **The coefficient
    is 10%, not 8%.** `items_meraki.json` is FROZEN, not lagging: its body is byte-identical across
    all five vendored patch dirs (md5 `5f2ab2ca072637d9`, 16.10.1 .. 16.14.1) despite five separate
    fetches - the item-side instance of the RM-81 Meraki-pin defect. DDragon `items.json` in the
    SAME patch dir, CommunityDragon 16.14 and the wiki all read 10%, and the wiki's dated `V26.11`
    entry ("increased to 10% from 8%") predicts the exact 8 -> 10 flip observed between the vendored
    16.10.1 and 16.11.1 dirs. Meraki's 8% was correct for V25.04 .. V26.10. Its 30s per-target
    cooldown is still correct - only the coefficient moved. (2) **"folded next to
    `item_bonus_hp_amp_hp`" is under-specified** - there are TWO numerator regions, and this term
    joins the PERMANENT-HP family (`passive_health_hp` / `rune_perm_hp`), which the
    `_blend_with_heal` mirror does not carry, so it is folded into the main block ONLY. (3)
    **"copying the R46 rationale" is right for the MATH and wrong for the PLUMBING** -
    `assume_passive_health_stacks` never reaches `rank_items_by_ehp`, `hybrid.py` or `server.py`,
    so mirroring it would have shipped a lane unreachable from the scorer (same defect as
    `assume_kaenic_shield` / `assume_hsp_amp` / `assume_eclipse_shield` / `assume_chainlaced_shield`
    - that population is LARGER than the RM-104 note claims). R107/R136 plumbing was used instead.
    **DDragon's "(0s) per target" is a proven template artifact**, not a real cooldown: 23 items in
    16.14.1 render `(0s)` and exactly one renders a nonzero value, and Meraki supplies real
    cooldowns for four of the cohort. **The assumed-procs curve is the one judgement in the lane** -
    deliberately LOW per the R46 stack-count-proxy convention, zero below L7, 8 at L13 (above the
    ~5.2 that passes Warmog's, well below the ~16.5 that would take #1), specifically to avoid the
    RM-94 Mejai's pinned-at-max failure mode. **Arena mirror 223084 is tagged INHERITED, UNSOURCED**
    - no feed states a coefficient for it and Riot retuned that mirror on other axes (700 HP vs 900,
    2500g vs 3000g); re-source before any map-30 default-ON flip.

## 2026-07-19 - RM-107 LCU timeline fix + R136 rune health grants (relocated from ROADMAP.md)

_Relocated verbatim on the same ROADMAP.md size-budget pass as the RM-99 block above. Both slices are SHIPPED; the open siblings (RM-106, RM-106b, and RM-101's unbuilt remainder) deliberately stayed in ROADMAP.md._

### RM-107 (shipped `556662a7`)

    - **SHIPPED 2026-07-19. THE PRESCRIBED MECHANISM ABOVE WAS WRONG - do not inherit it.**
      "A narrowed except that logs a real WARN on a non-200" is mechanically impossible here:
      `_lcu_get` (`lcu/lcu_postgame_collector.py:891-906`) catches every exception ITSELF and
      returns `None`, so urllib's `HTTPError` for the 404 is consumed inside the helper and the
      outer `except Exception` never fired. The 404 arrived as `timeline is None`, failed the
      `isinstance` guard, and the function returned having logged NOTHING - not even the DEBUG
      line the filing describes. The fix is a status-aware sibling helper `_lcu_get_status`
      returning `(status, json)`; `_lcu_get` KEEPS its None-on-failure contract because seven
      other call sites depend on it and `_get_gameflow_phase` polls it where a non-200 is normal.
      The outer except is DELETED, not narrowed - after the rewrite nothing in the body can raise.
    - **HONEST VALUE: this restores ZERO item events, permanently.** `_save_item_events:562`
      filters to `ITEM_PURCHASED/SOLD/UNDO/DESTROYED`. **MEASURED 2026-07-19: the reduced event set
      is an LCU-TIMELINE property, not a KIWI property** - a PRACTICETOOL match on the same route
      returned 5 frames / 17 events, all `CHAMPION_KILL`, zero `ITEM_*`, exactly like KIWI's 88
      `CHAMPION_KILL` + 7 `BUILDING_KILL`. That settles RM-106a's first open question. All five
      `*_item_events` tables hold 0 rows and no production module reads them. The patch converts an
      invisible failure into a visible one and buys an unconsumed per-frame gold/xp payload; it does
      not restore item history. The zero-ITEM_* outcome is logged at INFO (not WARN) precisely
      because it is now known to be permanent and would otherwise fire after every game.
    - **A SECOND SILENT NO-OP, same file, found by the same probe and fixed in the same commit:**
      `_capture_via_history:755` requested `?begin=0&end=1`, which **measured HTTP 400** live. The
      supported spelling is `begIndex/endIndex` (`tools/lcu_agent.py:2025` already had it right;
      the two spellings contradicted each other in-repo). `_lcu_get` collapsed the 400 into `None`,
      so the EOG match-history fallback returned `False` for its whole life.
    - **RM-106a QUESTION (b) ANSWERED - the LCU window is HARD-CAPPED AT 20 ENTRIES.** Measured
      `endIndex=20/40/100/200` all return exactly 20 rows. So this route is recent-matches-only and
      **cannot backfill** the ~2952-match history. Frame nesting is TOP-LEVEL `frames` (no `info`
      wrapper). If a backfill is ever wanted, RM-106's SGP adapter is the only candidate left.

### RM-101 / R136 (shipped, ENGINE 1.225.0)

  - **R136 (2026-07-19, ENGINE 1.225.0) SHIPPED three of these: Overgrowth 8451 + Grasp 8437
    self-side (`_rune_health_grants.py`, DEFAULT-OFF `apply_rune_health_grants`) and Revitalize
    8453's flat half (`_rune_hsp_amp.py`, DEFAULT-OFF `apply_rune_hsp_amp`).** Both reuse the
    R132 `rune_ids` transport, so no entry point gained a new ids parameter. Revitalize's
    "10% stronger below 40% health" clause is deliberately unmodelled (target-state, CLOSED).

## 2026-07-19 - RM-102 / RM-103 / RM-105 research narratives (relocated from ROADMAP.md)

_All three SHIPPED in the ENGINE 1.227.0 batch. Relocated verbatim on the same
size-budget pass. RM-104 stayed in ROADMAP.md - it is still open._

### RM-102

- **RM-102 `_item_bonus_hp_amp.py:89` credits Warmog's Vitality 0.12 to Arena mirror 443083, which
  has no Vitality passive at all.** Verified against the raw index: base `3083` carries "Warmog's
  Vitality: Gain bonus Health equal to 12% of your Item Health", mirror `443083` carries only
  Warmog's Heart regen and 4% move speed. This is not a wrong magnitude like R133 - it credits an
  effect the item does not have. Same "base nominal" seeding root cause as R133, different
  registry. S, value-only.

### RM-103

- **RM-103 Unending Despair 2502 self-heal half is uncredited AND mislabelled.** Meraki "Anguish":
  sap nearby champions every 4s for 3% bonus health magic damage AND "heal yourself equal to 250%
  of the post-mitigation damage dealt". `_effects_data.py` models the damage and calls the rest
  "ally self-heal component utility-only" - wrong on both counts (it is a SELF heal, and it is a
  2.5x multiplier per champion hit, not utility). `heal_total` lands in the EHP NUMERATOR of all
  three per-type denominators, so it is resist-amplified. Counterfactual measured at the most
  conservative setting (1 champ in range, 1 trigger per fight window): UD moves #6-8 -> #4 on
  Sion / Ornn / Zac / Leona / Malphite / Sejuani. Fix shape: `_item_proc_heal.py` sibling of
  `_item_omnivamp.py`, folded into `heal_item_total` behind DEFAULT-OFF `assume_item_proc_heal`.
  S, **no schema lift** - `ItemHeal.bonus_hp_scaling` already exists and already resolves, it
  simply has ZERO carriers today (all four `ItemHeal(` entries are AD-scaled).

### RM-105

- **RM-105 `effective_ehp_with_sustain` silently understates whenever ANY permanent-HP-family flag
  is ON - measured 2026-07-19 during the R137 build, pre-existing, NOT a DS-sweep GAP id.**
  `ehp.py` assembles its per-type numerators TWICE: the main block, and the `_blend_with_heal`
  mirror that produces `effective_ehp_with_sustain`. The mirror carries `ext_flat_hp` +
  `item_mana_health_hp` + `item_bonus_hp_amp_hp` + `flat_mit_*` but OMITS the entire PERMANENT-HP
  family - `passive_health_hp` (R46), `rune_perm_hp` (R136) and now `item_health_stack_hp` (R137).
  So the two fields diverge by exactly the omitted HP the moment any of those seams is armed.
  Measured live on Sion L13 `[3084, 3068]`: `apply_rune_health_grants` ON gives blended 6283.41 vs
  sustain 5665.08 (**-618.33**, ~10 percent); `assume_passive_health_stacks` ON gives -301.42;
  `assume_item_health_stacks` ON gives -373.24; at defaults the delta is exactly 0.0000.
  **Nothing catches it:** `test_ehp_sustain_contract.py`'s
  `test_effective_equals_blended_when_no_spellvamp_omnivamp` asserts the equality only at DEFAULT
  flags, and the mirror's own comment claims it mirrors the main numerators - which was true when
  written and stopped being true at R46. **This bites exactly when the standing plan executes:**
  every one of these seams is queued for an operator-gated default-ON flip, and the flip is what
  arms the divergence. Fix shape: add the three terms to `_blend_with_heal` and widen the contract
  test to assert the equality with each seam ARMED, not just at defaults. S, no schema lift. R137
  deliberately followed the family precedent (main-only) rather than widening the divergence with a
  fourth term - correcting the family is this item, not that one.

  the damage and says "(heal + permanent-HP sides not modeled)"; note the coefficients ALREADY

  Font of Life's base heal is an unresolved `@BaseHeal@` template var in live DDragon 16.14.1, a

    self-side (`_rune_health_grants.py`) and Revitalize 8453's flat half
    (`_rune_hsp_amp.py`), both DEFAULT-OFF and both reusing the R132 `rune_ids`
    transport. Detail relocated to `docs/ROADMAP_HISTORY.md`; the engine-side
    record is the ENGINE 1.225.0 entry in `agents/daemon_slayer/CHANGELOG.md`.
  - **TWO CLAIMS IN THE PARAGRAPH ABOVE ARE REFUTED - do not inherit them.** (1) Second Wind
    does NOT need a missing-health convention: `ehp.py:355 _MISSING_HP_SHARE_FOR_HEALS = 0.5`
    is a local variable at the exact call site, and `ability_hps.py:254` carries a second

    _ASSUMED_FLAT_DR_INSTANCES = 6.0` with discrete instance math at `:270` shipped in R9 on

    SELF half ("both of you gain a shield") the R132 rejection note missed. A self-only,
